\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, train_triples: Optional[set[tuple]] = None, train_facts: Optional[set[tuple]] = None):\\\\n        self.train_triples = train_triples or doc.get(\\\\\\\"title\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n            vertex_set = doc[\\\\\\\"vertexSet\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            for label in doc.get(\\\\\\\"labels\\\\\\\", []):\\\\n                h_name = vertex_set[label[\\\\\\\"h\\\\\\\"]][0][\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                t_name = vertex_set[label[\\\\\\\"t\\\\\\\"]][0][\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                rel_name = DOCRED_REL_INFO.get(label[\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\"], label[\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\"])\\\\n                train_triples.add(\\\\n                    _triple_key(doc_id, h_name, t_name, rel_name)\\\\n                )\\\\n                train_facts.add((h_name.lower().strip(), t_name.lower().strip(), rel_name.lower().strip()))\\\\n\\\\n        logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loaded %d training triples (%d unique facts) for             \\\"doc_id\\\": doc_id,\\n                \\\"head\\\": h_name,\\n                \\\"tail\\\": t_name,\\n                \\\"relation\\\": DOCRED_REL_INFO.get(label[\\\"r\\\"], (doc_id, head.lower().strip(), tail.lower().strip(), relation.lower().strip())\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass DocREDEvaluator:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"DocRED 评测器。\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        train_triples: 训练集中的三元组集合，用于计算 Ign-F1。\\\\n                       每个元素为 (doc_title, head_name, tail_name, relation)。\\\\n                       为 None 时 Ign-F1 = F1。\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, train_triples: Optional[set[tuple]] = None, train_facts: Optional[set[tuple]] = None):\\\\n        self.train_triples = train_triples or set()\\\\n        self.train_facts = train_facts or set()\\\\n\\\\n    @classmethod\\\\n    def from_train_file(cls, train_path: str) -> \\\\\\\"DocREDEvaluator\\\\\\\":\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"从训练集 JSON 文件构建 evaluator，自动提取训练集三元组。\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        from freige.data.docred_processor import DOCRED_REL_INFO\\\\n\\\\n        with open(train_path) as f:\\\\n            train_data = json.load(f)\\\\n\\\\n        train_triples = set()\\\\n        train_facts = set()\\\\n        for doc in train_data:\\\\n            doc_id = doc.get(\\\\\\\"title\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n            vertex_set = doc[\\\\\\\"vertexSet\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            for label in doc.get(\\\\\\\"labels\\\\\\\", []):\\\\n                h_name = vertex_set[label[\\\\\\\"h\\\\\\\"]][0][\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                t_name = vertex_set[label[\\\\\\\"t\\\\\\\"]][0][\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                rel_name = DOCRED_REL_INFO.get(label[\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\"], label[\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\"])\\\\n                train_triples.add(\\\\n                    _triple_key(doc_id, h_name, t_name, rel_name)\\\\n                )\\\\n                train_facts.add((h_name.lower().strip(), t_name.lower().strip(), rel_name.lower().strip()))\\\\n\\\\n        logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loaded %d training triples (%d unique facts) for predictions: list[dict],\\\\n        gold: list[dict],\\\\n    ) -> dict:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"计算 F1 和 Ign-F1。\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        pred_set = set()\\\\n        for p in 保存 metrics.json\\n    metrics_path = output_dir / \\\"metrics.json\\\"\\n    with open(metrics_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Metrics → %s\\\", metrics_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台汇总\\n    if args.split == \\\"test\\\":\\n        print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n        print(f\\\"  Test Documents: {n_total}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Predictions:    {len(all_predictions)}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  CodaLab file:   {len(codalab_preds)} entries\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Format OK:      {n_format_ok}/{n_total} ({metrics.get('format_ok_rate', 0):.1%})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Truncated:      {n_truncated}/{n_total}\\\")\\n        print(\\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n    else:\\n        print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n        print(f\\\"  F1:          {metrics['f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Ign-F1:      {metrics['ign_f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Evi-F1:      {metrics['evi_f1']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  EDCR:        {metrics.get('edcr', 0):.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Format OK:   {n_format_ok}/{n_total} ({metrics['format_ok_rate']:.1%})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Truncated:   {n_truncated}/{n_total}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Predictions: {len(all_predictions)}\\\")\\n        print(\\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.41}}"}}}

Looking at their code, they're counting all predicted evidence in the denominator regardless of correctness, then only crediting matches when the triple itself is correct. That's exactly what our joint metric does, so the comparison is apples-to-apples—we're genuinely underperforming at 0.31 versus their 0.54.\n\nThe misleading 0.80+ number came from only evaluating evidence on correct triples, which hides the damage from false positives and negatives. But there's another factor dragging down our score: the evidence_format_rate is only 70% for SFT, meaning 30% of documents have malformed evidence fields that contribute empty predictions, tanking both precision and recall.\n\nI should also check if there's a subtle difference in how we're computing this—DREEAM indexes entities by position while we use entity names, which could affect whether we're actually measuring the same thing. Looking at DREEAM's `re_eval` function more carefully, I see it iterates through sorted predictions and checks if each one matches a gold pair, then accumulates evidence accuracy across all predictions regardless of whether the relation itself was correct. This confirms that both `re_eval` and `official_evaluate` use the same evidence F1 calculation protocol. I'm looking at the evidence evaluation logic in the official DocRED evaluation function—it counts all predicted evidence regardless of whether the relation triple itself is correct, then calculates precision and recall based on how much the predicted evidence overlaps with the gold standard evidence. This matches what our `compute_evi_f1_joint` function does, so we're on the right track with the existing metrics.\n\nThe real problem is that the reported metric has been `evi_f1` (which inflates scores by only counting evidence from correct predictions), not `evi_f1_joint`. I need to verify our implementation is truly correct and then create a comparison table showing the difference between the two metrics.\n\nLooking more closely at the implementations, there's a subtle difference in how duplicates are handled. Our approach unions evidence sets when the same triple appears multiple times, whereas DREEAM filters duplicates by keeping only the highest-scoring one. In practice this shouldn't matter since our model rarely produces exact duplicate triples, but I'm checking the deduplication logic...\n\nExamining DREEAM's deduplication more carefully...\n\nThe key insight is that our `evi_f1` metric isn't actually DREEAM-compatible because it only counts true positives, which inflates the scores. Our `evi_f1_joint` metric is the one that matches DREEAM's evaluation, and when I look at the actual numbers, the gap is substantial—our best results are around 0.37 compared to DREEAM's 0.54.
